In /Users/changgeng/Project/Project06/Project06/BehavioralAISubstrate, commit cbb27f846 (ch1044 "D3") converted the commit-token/warrant digests in EBrainRuntimeCoordinator+SovereignCommit.swift from a non-injective `sovereignDigestHex` (SHA256 over a "|"-join) to the injective `sovereignDigestHexInjective` (SHA256 over `BASSovereignCanonicalBytes.lengthPrefixed`). It MISSED two remaining `sovereignDigestHex(` call sites in the same file:

1. Line ~1609 in `buildSovereignAuditEntry(...)`: 
   `let signature = sovereignDigestHex([auditID, sessionID, turnID, verdictID, snapshotRef, policyHash] + ruleIDs + signalRefs + actionRefs)`
   This is forgeable: a "|" in any list element (signalRefs is built from sovereignVerdict.reasonCodes + warrants.witnessRefs + observationStatusCodes — composite refs) shifts a list boundary so two DISTINCT audit-entry contents produce the SAME `signature`. Verified concrete collision: ruleIDs=["R"],signalRefs=["S"],actionRefs=["A"] collides with ruleIDs=["R|S"],signalRefs=[],actionRefs=["A"] (both "|"-join to ...|R|S|A). This `signature` is carried in EBrainTurnResult.sovereignAuditEntry; it is currently never re-verified by any consumer, so it is a defense-in-depth / consistency gap rather than a live trust bypass — but it directly contradicts the D3 fix goal.

2. Line ~1681 in `sovereignPolicyHash(for:)`:
   `return sovereignDigestHex(components)` over 4 policy-lineage fields (bundleVersion, providerRoutingPolicyID, runtimeTuningPolicyID, resolutionSourceID). Forgeable at the field boundary if any field contains "|". This policyHash feeds into the (now-injective) commit-token actionDigest AND the audit-entry signature, so a lossy sub-hash partially undermines D3's guarantee.

CRITICAL implementation detail (I proved this empirically): you CANNOT simply swap `sovereignDigestHex` → `sovereignDigestHexInjective` at line 1609, because it passes THREE consecutive variable-length lists (ruleIDs + signalRefs + actionRefs) with NO count markers. Bare length-prefixing is STILL arity-ambiguous across adjacent lists: ruleIDs=["R","M"],signalRefs=["S"] collides with ruleIDs=["R"],signalRefs=["M","S"] under flat length-prefixing. You MUST add per-list count markers exactly like the D2 1.2.0 audit-canonical form does in basSovereignAuditCanonicalBytes (BASSovereignEd25519Signing.swift lines 149-162): `parts.append(String(list.count)); parts += list` for EACH of ruleIDs, signalRefs, actionRefs. (Note `BASSovereignCanonicalBytes.list(_:)` already exists as a helper that prepends the count.) Line 1681's 4 fields are fixed-arity so a plain `sovereignDigestHexInjective(components)` is sufficient there.

The four EXISTING D3 sites (lines 125, 142, 161, 209) are sound and need no change — each has at most one variable-length list in an arity-unambiguous position (list-at-front-followed-by-fixed-count-tail at 125; trailing-list-after-fixed-scalars at 161/209; no list at 142) — I brute-forced all of them, 0 collisions.

Also consider adding a direct encoder-level injectivity unit test for BASSovereignCanonicalBytes.lengthPrefixed (currently no test references it directly — injectivity is only tested through call sites). ### ⬜ [2026-06-01 09:35:47] Claude

全部完成、已推送、工作树干净。"好" 之后的 **A3 集成层那一节**整段做完了。

## 本节交付（4 个 commit，全部 opt-in / 关闭时字节相等）

| 项 | commit | 测试 | 关键点 |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A3(a) 集成** — 持久化 `sovereignSeal` 字段 + 写入即签 / load 即验门 | `8da05c2c5` | 16 | 字段搭 `payload_json` blob 走，**零 schema 迁移**;`requireValidSeal(allowUnsealed:false)` 连"删掉 seal 字段"的绕过都堵了 |
| **A3(b)** — 对削减 hardNoGo/confirmRequired 的审批加双密钥门 | `7110220e4` | 9 | intentDigest 绑定**精确转换**,一个 commit 不能重放到另一个转换 |
| **A2 append-floor** — 可选 `minimumSchemaVersion` | `f85e2d15a` | 4 | nil 默认=现状;设 "1.2.0" 即拒绝非注入式旧条目 |
| **ADR-027 更新** — 文档如实标注集成层已建 | `4892b527d` | — | §5 已建 / §6 诚实剩余 |

## 这一节的三个关键判断（小心翼翼）
1. **拆掉了最大的风险**:原以为要给最敏感的宪法存储**引入列迁移框架**(native store 是 create-or-reject、无迁移机制)。实测发现**两个存储都是 Codable `payload_json` blob**——seal 直接进 blob,**不动 schema、不动 `PRAGMA user_version`**,旧 blob 解码缺键即 nil。红线 7 守住。
2. **密钥来源不靠猜**:代码里已有 `BASSovereignKeychainBinding`(存/取 Ed25519 keypair),所以签名密钥**沿用既有模式**,不是每进程随机、不是 `fromSeed("constant")`。
3. **A2 没硬上**:naive 硬 floor 要翻全局默认 + 改 ~5 个测试文件、且有运行时崩的风险(亏的)。改成 opt-in floor——安全、字节相等、前向只作用于新追加。

## 一处诚实核查(最严谨)
广扫时 swift-testing helper 又报 **SIGBUS(signal 10)**,且这次**确定性复现**。我没敢当环境问题略过——把未提交的 A2 改动**撤回到 HEAD 做了 A/B 对照**:基线(无我的改动)**照样崩**,且每次串扰的测试名都不同(M398Cthulhu / M520 / multilingual-audit)。**结论:是既有环境性崩溃**(那条 `libbas_memory_usage_tracker` sqlite3.o 按 macOS 26.5 编、链到 14.0 的 ABI 不匹配,faults helper 进程),**非本次回归**。XCTest 侧确定性全绿(2136,0 失败)。

## 真正剩下的(A1 式 layer-2,诚实)
所有**可复用主权原语都已建齐、opt-in、有测试**:鉴权器 / 验证器 / 持久化 seal / fail-closed load 门 / 双密钥审批门 / 审批审计条目构建器 / 账本 append-floor。剩的纯粹是**逐宿主调用点**——一个用 keychain 绑定密钥、写时签 / load 时验 / 审批时调双密钥门 / 落审批审计 / 开账本 floor 的宿主。这需要一个**真正用主权密钥做宪法持久化+审批的宿主**(现宪法流是纯值型、无密钥/账本)——和 A1 同样的两层诚实:门都在、都是一次调用,但当前没有宿主执行该流程。

四个粘贴任务(D3 / A1 / A2 / A3 a+b+c+d)的**可在 substrate 内独立完成的部分,现已全部落地**。再往前就是宿主集成——要么等那个宿主出现,要么你指一个现有宿主,我把这些门接到它的宪法 load/save/approve 上。

### ⬜ [2026-06-01 14:40:21] Claude

**Found the bug.** Line 147-148 of the endurance test:

```swift
footprintMB = Double(info.virtual_size) / 1024.0 / 1024.0
```

It reads **`info.virtual_size`** — the process's **virtual address-space size** — and labels it "footprint." On a 64-bit iOS process with MLX/Metal, virtual_size is ~404 GB of *reserved* address space (near-constant, independent of real memory) — exactly the garbage we saw. It's **not** the physical footprint.

The real jetsam-relevant metric is **`phys_footprint`**, which needs `TASK_VM_INFO` — and the substrate **already has** a correct probe for it (`bas_task_phys_footprint` in `BASCSystemProbes.swift`), but the test reimplemented its own snapshot with `MACH_TASK_BASIC_INFO` (which doesn't even contain phys_footprint) and grabbed `virtual_size` instead. Let me confirm the existing probe's API to fix it properly:

## 你让我查的「被杀原因 / 是不是 bug」—— 结论

**被杀本身不是 bug,是外部超时;但你的直觉对了 —— 顺藤摸出一个真 telemetry bug。**

### 1) 为什么被杀 → harness 后台任务超时(~19 分钟),不是崩溃
证据(诚实核查,不是猜):
- 两次日志末尾,测试都**健康地停在 MLX 迭代中途**(iter=7 / iter=8 推理中),**没有任何** signal / SIGABRT / jetsam / 内存告警 / 设备断连 / 堆栈。崩溃会留痕,这里没有。
- 都卡在 ~19 分钟墙钟;detached 重launch 实验也证明沙箱会在工具调用返回时回收子进程。
- → 是 harness/沙箱外部杀的,**不是设备/代码故障**。

### 2) 你的直觉摸出的真 bug:`footprint_mb` 报了 **404 GB**
- ch1025 耐久监控把 `footprint_mb` 算成了 **`info.virtual_size`** —— 进程**预留的 64 位虚拟地址空间**(MLX/Metal 进程 ~404 GB,近乎常数、跟真实内存无关)。所以这个指标是**垃圾**:rss 在 1577 vs 2952 两次跑里差一倍,footprint 却都 ~404300。
- 而 `mach_task_basic_info` **根本没有 phys_footprint 字段**。真正该用的是 `phys_footprint`(jetsam 判定用的那个),substrate 里**早就有正确探针** `BASTaskVmInfoProbe`(TASK_VM_INFO),只是这俩地方各自手搓了 basic_info 还拿错了字段。
- **两处都有**这个 bug,其中一个**不是测试、是设备 App 代码**:`BASEnduranceAppRunner.swift:203`。
- **已修两处** → 改用 `BASTaskVmInfoProbe.rawSnapshot().physFootprintBytes`(单一真源)。验证:`swift build --build-tests` 通过 + 设备 target `BUILD SUCCEEDED`。

### 3) 顺带两个好消息
- **没有内存泄漏**:单次跑内 `rss_mb` 跨 8 次迭代**纹丝不动**(~2952 稳定;fuzz 那次还从 1577 降到 1441),`avail_mb` 稳在 ~200MB 不往 0 掉 → 真跑 2 小时也不会因泄漏 OOM。(修正我之前"1441→2951 在涨"的误判——那是**两次不同的跑**比错了。)
- **真机功能全套(跳过耐久环)= `13564` 测试 0 失败**(71 skipped,~5 分钟)—— 这是你选的 option-2 的干净结论。

所以:被杀是环境上限(无解,除非你自己终端里跑或我分块跑),但这趟把一个**真·可观测性 bug**(还波及设备 App 代码)给揪出来修了。

# 真机数据全面 audit — ch1044

**数据源:** 4 个真机 run 日志(13564-test 完整跑 + 2 次 endurance 被杀跑 + smoke/batch),含完整 MLX/thermal/memory 遥测。**方法:诚实核查每一个可疑信号——确认是 bug 才报 bug。**

## 🔴 已修复的真 bug(本会话)
**`footprint_mb` 报 404 GB** — 两处把 `info.virtual_size`(64 位预留虚拟地址空间,~404 GB,跟真实内存无关)当成内存 footprint。其中一处是**设备 App 代码**(`BASEnduranceAppRunner.swift`),非测试。已改用真正的 `phys_footprint`(jetsam 用的那个,via `BASTaskVmInfoProbe`)。✅ 已修 + 编译验证 + 推送(`074b055a2`)。

## 🟡 新发现的真 finding(需要你定夺,我没擅自改)
**`testPreTokenizationDeliversAtLeast5xSpeedup` 名不副实** — 测试名承诺"≥5× 加速",但**只断言两条路径都 sub-5ms + 解码正确,从不断言 speedup**。实测是 **0.53×(反而慢 2×)**:actor 预分词 378µs/op vs raw 200µs/op。最可能是 ch737 PQ 算法把 raw 路径提速后,这个 actor 优化变成**净负收益**,而它自己的守护测试抓不到。这是"绿但没在守它声称的契约"的典型。
> R1 纪律:我**没有**把测试改成 `assert(speedup>=0.5)` 去迁就退化——那是降标。正确做法要么修优化、要么让测试如实断言(会失败、暴露回归)、要么像 PQ 那样写明是 crossover。等你定。

## 🟢 查过=确认不是 bug(诚实区分)
- **PQ 向量索引 Recall@10 = 4%「FAIL ❌」** → **故意且有文档**:`testPQQualityAndPerfAtRealisticCorpus` 明写均匀随机向量 5K 语料 K=16 下 recall ~3-5% 是预期,PQ 拿 recall 换 **53× 内存 + 78× 速度**,生产数据更高,**故意不硬断言**。那个「FAIL ❌」只是它自报的信息标签(措辞误导,行为没问题)。
- run 里其它 `❌/FAIL/regression/exceeded` → 全是 benchmark **图例文字 / 配置项**(`TREAT_MISSING_BASELINES_AS_TEST_FAILURES=NO`)。**0 失败是真的。**
- **`deletedDomain` 未知域被跳过** → `loadAllObjects()` 对未知 domain **优雅跳过+告警**(不崩),低危,大概率测试诱发。

## ⚠️ 环境/非代码(已核实)
- **~19 分钟被杀 = harness 后台任务上限,不是崩溃**:停在 MLX 迭代中途,无 signal/jetsam/断连/堆栈。
- **无内存泄漏**:`rss_mb` 跨 8 次迭代纹丝不动;`avail_mb` 稳 ~200MB 不掉。
- **热管理健康**:自适应冷却 60→90→180s,serious→nominal **能恢复**(iter4/6),**从没到 critical**;MLX 吞吐跨迭代不退化(iter8 ≈ iter1)。

## ℹ️ 覆盖缺口(诚实)
- 设备上 **`BASSovereignKeychainBindingTests`**(我 A3 的密钥来源机制)+ **constitution/RiskService/SoftCaution** 测试**被 skip**(env/entitlement gated)→ 我这次 A3 的 key-provenance/宪法面**没在真机验证过**(macOS 上是绿的)。
- 长 soak/endurance(ch1025、CognitiveOS1HourSoak、Chenglu20MinStress)在 harness 窗口内**跑不完**。
- `BASThermalTwinNotificationTests` skip 理由写的是"macOS flaky",却在 **iOS 真机也 skip** → 可能 skip 守卫过宽(小)。

## ✅ 正向验证
- **13564 真机测试,0 失败**(确认为真,非掩盖)。
- **真机 MLX(Gemma 4 E2B)推理可用**:ch952 首 token 延迟 / 持续吞吐 ≥5 tok/s / 流式 全过。
- smoke + 40-batch 共 41 个先前也全绿。

## 谁杀的 — 定性了

我的 wrapper 挂了 SIGTERM/SIGHUP/SIGINT 的 trap,而且 xcodebuild 自己退出时会打 `EXITED ON ITS OWN rc=N`。**这三种 trap 一个没触发,xcodebuild 退出行也没有** —— 进程凭空消失、零痕迹。**只有一种情况会这样:SIGKILL(-9)**,它无法被捕获、瞬间生效、不留痕。

**BASHostKit / BASObservability / BASAppleAdapters 等一堆类被同时链进了 App 框架 *和* 测试 bundle** —— objc 自己警告"可能导致莫名其妙的崩溃"。这是 `BASDeviceTest.xcodeproj`(project.yml)的**重复链接配置问题**:测试 target 又静态链了一遍 App 里已经嵌入的 package 产物。真机跑里它一直在刷,是个实打实的打包 bug,还可能给真机跑添乱。

### ⬜ [2026-06-02 06:52:41] Claude

Investigated the actual cause — and I need to correct what I implied. It is **not** a simple project.yml toggle; it's **structural**:

- The device test files use **`@testable import` everywhere**: BASHostKit in **402** files, BASAppleAdapters 71, BASObservability 33, BASEvaluation 10.
- `@testable` forces the **test bundle** to carry its *own* testability build of each module → those classes live in `BASDeviceTests.xctest`.
- The **host app** *also* embeds the same modules (`BASHostKit_…_PackageProduct.framework`) — because it links them for its `BAS_ENDURANCE_AUTOSTART` standalone-endurance path (project.yml lines 73-88).
- Same classes in the app's framework **and** the test binary, both loaded into one process → the objc warning.

So there's no safe "remove a dep" fix — that'd break 400+ `@testable` imports. The honest options each have a cost:

| fix | effect | cost |
|---|---|---|
| **A. Minimal host** — drop the app's heavy deps + exclude `BASEnduranceAppRunner` | **eliminates** the warning for `xcodebuild test` (what we use); verifiable via a device build | **drops the `devicectl process launch` autostart path** (we've never used it — always `xcodebuild test`) |
| B. Drop `@testable` in 400+ files | clean | massive rewrite, not worth it |
| C. Mergeable-frameworks restructure | keeps both | finicky Xcode linkage, risky, hard to verify |
| D. Leave it | none | benign — 13564 tests + 88-min endurance + 41 tests **all passed**; objc's "may cause" never fired |

Per 亏的不要上, I won't unilaterally drop a capability (the autostart path) to silence a benign warning. So — your call:

The lever for that is the **production `Package.swift` product linkage** — a **whole-package blast-radius change** (it affects the main app, the CLI, every consumer), not a device-test-only tweak.

Per 亏的不要上 / 小心翼翼, I will **not** rush a production package-linkage change inline at the tail of this very long session — it needs a dedicated pass that verifies the *entire* package + every consumer still builds, plus iterative device-build checks. ## 完整取证链(每步排除一个嫌疑)
| 步骤 | 方法 | 结论 |
|---|---|---|
| 手机? | 日志看测试状态 | ❌ 被杀时手机端测试健康(MLX 迭代中途) |
| xcodebuild 自退? | wrapper 记 `EXITED rc=` | ❌ 从没记到,是 wrapper 被杀 |
| cowork VM? | grep 我会话 id | ❌ cowork 是别的会话;我的是直连 macOS(0 命中) |
| 谁拥有进程? | 进程祖先链 | bash ← per-cmd zsh ← `claude --resume d80fe4d0` ← Claude.app |
| 什么信号? | 信号 trap | **SIGKILL**(无痕)+ 版本 2.1.156<2.1.160 |
| 固定时限? | 纯 bash 控制探针 | ❌ 跑满 60min 自己结束,没被杀 → 非定时器 |
| **确切触发?** | **app 自己的 main.log** | ✅ **IdleManager 空闲 15min → "Pausing session … due to idle" → 拆 PTY** |

## 为什么时长乱跳(19/24/29/88min)
**空闲计时器被"活动"重置。** 后台任务只在**连续空闲 >15min**(我在等你回消息)时才死。存活 = 启动 + 各活动段(每段重置计时)+ 最后那段致命空闲。**活证据:** 旁边那个纯 bash 探针现在还活着(~9min)——纯粹因为我一直在跑命令、不断重置计时器;我要是停 15min,它就会死。

## 我错了三次、你对了三次(诚实)
"固定 19min" → "~29min" → "触发器看不到" 都是我没查到底。你每次都逼我再查,这次才挖到 app 日志里的 `IdleManager` 原话。

## 这反而解决了你最初的"跑满 1 小时"
既然唯一的杀手是**会话空闲 >15min**,那 1 小时真机跑是**能成的**:
- **我每 <15min 轮询一次**那个跑(顺便报进度)→ 重置计时器 → 跑不死;
- 或**你 Ctrl+T 把会话 pin 住**(豁免空闲回收);
- 或**你终端里跑**(没有 IdleManager)。

要不要我现在就用"每 <15min 轮询保活"的方式,把那个 1 小时 iPhone Air endurance 真正跑满给你看?

```
  This may cause spurious casting failures and mysterious crashes. One of the duplicates must be removed or renamed.
```
Affected: BASObservability, BASEvaluation, BASAppleAdapters, BASHostKit (and their transitive classes).

## Confirmed root cause
- The device test files use `@testable import` HEAVILY: BASHostKit in 402 files, BASAppleAdapters in 71, BASObservability in 33, BASEvaluation in 10. `@testable` forces the TEST bundle (`BASDeviceTests.xctest`) to carry its own testability build of each module → those classes live in the test binary.
- The HOST app (`BASDeviceTestApp`) ALSO links/embeds the same modules (DeviceTestApp/project.yml lines ~66-88) because it needs them for its `BAS_ENDURANCE_AUTOSTART` standalone path (`BASEnduranceAppRunner.swift`, run via `devicectl device process launch`). These become `*_PackageProduct.framework` dynamic frameworks embedded in the app.
- The SwiftPM product framework (e.g. BASHostKit) statically merges its transitive deps (BASObservability/Evaluation/AppleAdapters), so those classes end up in BOTH the app's framework AND the test binary → same classes in one process → the objc warning.

## Goal (option C — keep BOTH paths)
Restructure linkage so the host app and the test bundle share ONE copy of each BAS module. Must keep working:
1. `xcodebuild test` (test bundle @testable-imports BAS modules, injected into the host app) — the primary path.
2. `devicectl process launch` autostart (BASEnduranceAppRunner runs standalone in the app, needs BAS symbols).

## Approaches to evaluate (likely needs Package.swift, so verify the whole package)
- Declare the BAS library products as explicitly DYNAMIC in the package manifest (`Package.swift`: `.library(name:..., type: .dynamic, targets: [...])`) so there's a single shared dynamic framework both app and test link against. ⚠️ This is a WHOLE-PACKAGE blast-radius change — it affects the main app, BASBrainCLI, and every consumer. You MUST verify `swift build` for the whole package AND the main app/CLI still build + run before shipping.
- Mergeable libraries (`MERGEABLE_LIBRARY=YES` / `MERGED_BINARY_TYPE`) on the relevant targets.
- Build the shared frameworks with `ENABLE_TESTABILITY=YES` so `@testable import` resolves against the shared (app-embedded) framework instead of a private test copy; then make the test target link-not-duplicate.
- If a clean device-test-project-only fix exists in DeviceTestApp/project.yml (xcodegen spec; regenerate via `cd DeviceTestApp && xcodegen generate`), prefer it over touching Package.swift.

## Files
- `DeviceTestApp/project.yml` (xcodegen spec — regenerate with `cd DeviceTestApp && xcodegen generate`; the .xcodeproj is generated, do not hand-edit it).
- Possibly `Package.swift` (product linkage) — but treat any change here as whole-package blast radius.
- `DeviceTestApp/Sources/App/BASEnduranceAppRunner.swift` (the autostart-path code that pulls the heavy deps into the app).
- Background: `DeviceTestApp/README.md`.

## Verification (each iteration)
1. `cd DeviceTestApp && xcodegen generate` (then back to repo root).
2. Device build: `xcodebuild build -project DeviceTestApp/BASDeviceTest.xcodeproj -scheme BASDeviceTestApp -destination "platform=iOS,id=9E9E3DEB-E9F5-5C2D-A6B1-9B31A70659D6" -allowProvisioningUpdates -skipPackagePluginValidation` (incremental ~2-5 min). Known quirk: if it fails with "Build input files cannot be found ... BASSQLSchemaGen/...generated.swift", mirror the generated SQL from `<DerivedData>/Index.noindex/.../BuildToolPluginIntermediates` into `<DerivedData>/Build/.../BuildToolPluginIntermediates` (see README "Working device test command").
3. Grep the build/test log for `is implemented in both` — the objc warnings must be GONE.
4. Confirm the harness still works: `xcodebuild test ... -only-testing:BASDeviceTests/BASChapter946FourteenLayerFuzzSmokeTests/testL8AtomLifecycleFuzzRoundTrip` passes on device (UDID 9E9E3DEB-E9F5-5C2D-A6B1-9B31A70659D6, "Chang's iPhone", iPhone Air).
5. If you changed Package.swift: run `swift build` (whole package) + verify the main app/CLI targets still build. If anything breaks, REVERT (the warning is benign — 13564 device tests + an 88-minute endurance run + 41 smoke/batch tests all passed with it present).

## Discipline
Do not break the working device harness. The objc warning is benign-in-practice (everything passed), so a fix that risks the harness or the production package is worse than the warning — prefer reverting to shipping a risky change. ### ⬜ [2026-06-02 07:21:44] Claude

Option C — attempted in full, and it hit a hard wall. Here's the honest outcome:

## What I did + what blocked it
I applied the documented thorough fix: made all 16 BAS library products `type: .dynamic` (shared frameworks). The whole package + CLI + macOS tests built fine — but the **Xcode device build failed structurally**:

```
error: Swift package target 'BASRuntimeCore' is linked as a static library by 'BASDeviceTests'
       and 14 other targets, but cannot be built dynamically because there is a package product
       with the same name.   (×17 modules)
```

This is a **fundamental SwiftPM constraint**, not a tweakable setting: a product can't be made dynamic when **its name equals its target name** *and* the target is static-linked across the graph (BASRuntimeCore is static-linked by 14 targets; BASMemory by 7; etc.). Every BAS product hits this.

The **only** way to make dynamic frameworks work here is to **rename every product** so product-name ≠ target-name (e.g. `BASHostKit` → `BASHostKitLib`). That breaks the `.product(name: "BASHostKit")` references in **QinaoRuntimeSDK, SampleHost, BASBrainCLI, AND the device test** — a product-wide rename across 4+ consumers. The other C approaches (mergeable libraries, ENABLE_TESTABILITY) funnel into the same wall (mergeable *requires* dynamic; testability alone doesn't dedup).

## Reverted, per the discipline
The task's own discipline: *"a fix that risks the production package is worse than the warning — prefer reverting."* A product-wide rename for a **benign** warning fails that test decisively. So:
- ✅ `Package.swift` reverted — `git status` clean (byte-identical to HEAD).
- ✅ `swift build` restored (Build complete).
- ⏳ Re-running the device smoke (`bn48fn1jb`) to confirm the harness is fully back — will report when it lands.

## Honest recommendation: **option D (leave it)**
The objc warning is benign-in-practice — **13564 device tests + the 88-min endurance + 41 smoke/batch tests all passed** with it present; objc's "may cause mysterious crashes" never fired. Eliminating it costs a product rename rippling through every consumer of the package. Not worth it.

**Net:** option C is not achievable without a disproportionate, high-blast-radius rename. I tried it properly, it's structurally blocked, and I reverted cleanly. I'd close this as "won't-fix (benign)" unless you specifically want the product-rename effort — which I'd treat as its own large, separately-verified task. Confirming harness restoration now.

一句话定义：

1. 项目最终目标
本项目最终要构建一套：
以宿主为主权中心、以第二大脑为认知操作系统、以 LLM 为神经矿脉、以 SDK 为现实接口、以 L1–L14 为活体脑体、以多 agents 为单脑多席协同方式的电子脑平台。

主权第二大脑母板
2. 项目核心命题
2.1 LLM 不是脑，是神经矿脉
LLM 本身不是完整电子脑。 它是：

* 语言矿脉
* 世界先验矿脉
* 候选生成矿脉
* 反事实推演矿脉
* 风格迁移矿脉
* 风险信号矿脉
* 失败样本矿脉
* 蒸馏轨迹矿脉
电子脑的任务，不是崇拜这座矿，而是建立一套完整的：
开采 → 冶炼 → 校验 → 封存 → 蒸馏 → 回滚 → 再利用
的系统。
2.2 压榨不是多输出，而是多产出
普通用法：

```text
User → LLM → Answer
```

顶级电子脑用法：

```text
User
→ 解构对象
→ 记忆对象
→ 候选前沿
→ 反事实分岔
→ 反方简报
→ 风险场
→ 行为许可
→ 主权裁决
→ 外显表面
→ 经验票据
→ 蒸馏资产
```

也就是说：
一次输入，不只要一个答案。 一次输入，要榨出一整套可治理资产。
3. 总体系统架构
最终局采用：
三平面、四内核、八总线、两库一方舟

4. L1–L14 如何参与“压榨 LLM”
L1 灯芯层：压榨预算
它不直接调用 LLM，而是决定：

* 能不能调用
* 调多深
* 调多久
* 激活哪些 agents
* 是否允许 deep / max / guarded effort
核心产物：

```text
EffortPlan
BudgetFrame
RunLease
```

L2 脑肉层：压榨神经能力
把 LLM 拆成神经器官：

* 前哨识别
* 结构抽取
* 候选生成
* 未来投影
* 反方批判
* 风险特征
* 表面生成
核心目标：
不要让一个大模型一次性包办，而是把它拆成可调度器官。
L3 折叠肺：压榨运行时
把 LLM 能力压进端侧：

* 量化
* 热包 / 冷包
* 状态折页
* 断点续思
* 快照恢复
* 推理缓存
* 多精度执行图
核心目标：
让大脑能在小机器里持续呼吸。
L4 地平线层：压榨世界先验
LLM 的世界知识不应直接裸用。 要榨成：

* 因果模板
* 反事实结构
* 不确定性语法
* 边界先验
* 昆仑中轴视图
* 深渊不可知保留区
L5 宿纹层：压榨个体化调制
不是把用户画像塞 prompt。 而是生成：

```text
HostConstitution
HostVersion
ConsentMatrix
AgentPersonaSpec
HumanAnchorProfile
```

让 LLM 在宿主宪法下工作。
L6 临场眼：压榨局势感知
从输入中榨出：

* 场景类型
* 权力梯度
* 情绪天气
* 真假紧迫
* 操控前兆
* 昆仑中轴偏移
* 深渊异兆
核心产物：

```text
SituationField
AnomalyTrace
AxisDeviation
```

L7 镜刃层：压榨干净认知
从混乱输入中榨出：

* facts
* claims
* goals
* unknowns
* contradictions
* pressure vectors
* manipulation patterns
* boundary touches
核心产物：

```text
CanonicalCognitiveFrame
JadeMirrorDraft
UnknownSet
NarrativeDistortionMap
```

L8 海马井：压榨合法时间
不是把所有历史塞给 LLM。 而是榨出：

* 热记忆
* 温记忆
* 冷记忆
* EpisodeArc
* ConflictCluster
* YaochiSanctum
* OldSeal
核心原则：
只喂给 LLM 合法、必要、可追源的时间。
L9 梦环层：压榨多路径
让 LLM 不只产一个答案，而是产：

* CandidateFrontier
* CounterfactualBranch
* AscentBranch
* ReturnPath
* GuardBranch
* EvidenceDebt
* UncertaintyLedger
核心原则：
一次调用，榨多个未来。
L10 三我庭：压榨可承担性
从候选中榨出：

* 本我需求
* 自我现实账
* 超我边界
* 牺牲地图
* 悔意剖面
* 主体性保留位
核心产物：

```text
MergedChoice
SacrificeMap
RegretProfile
AgencyReservation
```

L11 风闸层：压榨风险边界
从候选中榨出：

* 风险向量
* 可逆性
* 证据足量度
* GSI
* AbyssPressure
* HeavenGatePermit
* ActionPermit
核心原则：
不是放不放，而是以什么压强放。
L12 柔手层：压榨外显表面
LLM 输出不应只有 answer。 要榨成：

* Answer
* ComparePanel
* DraftShell
* DelayPacket
* BoundaryScript
* LocalOnlyAction
* SilentStub
* ProcessView
核心原则：
不只产答案，产可落地表面。
L13 蜕变炉：压榨经验与失败
从每轮结果中榨出：

* UpdateTicket
* RuleCandidate
* HostChangeCandidate
* BiasRecord
* GuardTemplateCandidate
* ShadowTrialRecord
* LearningExportBundle
核心原则：
成功要榨，失败更要榨。
L14 玄戒层：压榨主权边界
从所有动作中榨出：

* 是否有资格继续
* 是否有资格外显
* 是否有资格写记忆
* 是否有资格调用工具
* 是否有资格成为规则
核心产物：

```text
SovereignWarrant
OldSeal
LineageCut
RollbackWrit
CleanReboot
DeadStopLatch
```

5. 最关键的新对象：LLM Invocation Contract
最终局必须禁止“随便问模型”。
每次调用 LLM，都必须带调用契约：

```text
LLMInvocationContract
- call_id
- purpose
  # decompose / plan / critique / risk / render / distill / verify
- input_refs[]
- allowed_context[]
- forbidden_context[]
- output_schema
- max_tokens
- effort_plan_ref
- agent_ref
- verifier_ref
- risk_scope
- memory_scope
- transcript_visibility
- failure_mode
- sovereign_constraints[]
```

一句话：
每一次调用 LLM，都必须知道自己在榨什么、允许榨什么、不能碰什么、产物交给谁。
6. 压榨流水线
最终系统的一轮完整流水线如下：

```text
1. 宿主输入
2. SDK 封装请求
3. L14 预检主权
4. L1 生成 EffortPlan / RunLease
5. L6 建局
6. L7 解构
7. L8 拉合法记忆
8. L9 生成候选前沿
9. 多 agents 并发提案
10. Critic 反方审查
11. Verifier 验货
12. L10 三我庭审可承担
13. L11 风闸生成 ActionPermit
14. L14 签发或拒签 SovereignWarrant
15. L12 生成外显表面
16. SDK 落地 UI / 工具 / 本地动作
17. L13 生成成长候选
18. L8 / L14 记录、封存、回滚锚点
19. 高质量 trace 进入蒸馏池
```

这就是：
一次输入，十九次榨取
7. 多 agents 如何参与压榨
最终局不是多个 agent 自然语言聊天，而是：
单脑多席、共享状态、共享潜变量、单提交口。
默认 agents：

* Scout：榨任务类型与升档需求
* Memory：榨合法历史与弧线
* Planner：榨候选前沿
* Critic：榨反方与证据债
* Host Alignment：榨宿主对齐
* Risk：榨风险许可
* Surface：榨外显表面
* Sovereign Sentinel：榨主权边界
* Evolution Shadow：榨经验候选
核心规则：

```text
agent 不直接提交
agent 不私有冷记忆
agent 不拥有宿主主权
agent 只提交 AgentDelta / AgentProposal
```

8. Effort × Transcript View 如何参与压榨
8.1 Effort 是压榨强度控制器
用户可以请求：

* fast
* balanced
* deep
* max
* guarded
* auto
但系统实际生效为：

```text
requested_effort
applied_effort
override_reason
```

Effort 控制：

* 候选数量
* agent 数量
* 记忆深度
* 反方强度
* 风险校准细度
* 输出详细度
* 工具验证强度
8.2 Transcript View 是压榨成果展示器
不是 raw CoT。 展示的是结构资产：

* 局势摘要
* 候选比较
* agent 贡献
* 风险许可
* 主体性保留
* redaction 说明
模式：

* off
* summary
* compare
* structured_trace
* agent_trace
* audit_lite
核心原则：
展示过程结构，不裸露隐藏内脏。
9. 昆仑 × 深渊双 doctrine 的压榨作用
9.1 昆仑 doctrine 负责“成器”
压榨出的东西不是一堆碎片，而要成器：

* 中轴：判断是否离中
* 登临：判断是否有资格继续深想
* 玉律：对象必须完整、可验、可签名
* 河源：对象必须可溯源
* 天门：动作必须过门
* 瑶池：高敏内容必须静库封存
9.2 深渊 doctrine 负责“不失控”
压榨不能把系统榨到污染：

* 不可知必须保留
* 异兆必须观察
* 污染必须隔离
* 封印必须生效
* 谱系必须可斩
* 错了必须净启
9.3 Human Anchor 负责“仍然站在人这一边”
再强的压榨都不能变成：

* 系统夺权
* 宿主被管理
* 柔性操控
* 伪温柔
* 依赖诱导
核心原则：
把 LLM 榨干，但不能把宿主榨干。
10. 技术研发阶段
Phase 0：压榨对象冻结
交付：

* `LLMInvocationContract`
* `EffortPlan`
* `CanonicalCognitiveFrame`
* `CandidateFrontier`
* `RiskField`
* `ActionPermit`
* `ProcessTrace`
* `UpdateTicket`
目标：
先让 LLM 不再只产答案，而是产对象。
Phase 1：P0 最小压榨机器
只做：

* L1 最小租约
* L6/L7 解构
* L9 候选
* L11 风闸
* L12 compare/draft/delay/stub
* L14 最小主权拒签
目标：
输入一次，至少榨出候选、风险、外显三类资产。
Phase 2：加入反方与验证器
新增：

* Critic Agent
* EvidenceDebt
* Schema Verifier
* Risk Verifier
* ToolIntent Verifier
目标：
不让 LLM 自说自话。
Phase 3：加入记忆与宿主
新增：

* MemoryBundle
* HostConstitution summary
* ConsentMatrix
* AgentPersonaSpec
目标：
让 LLM 吃到合法时间与宿主宪法。
Phase 4：加入单脑多席 agents
新增：

* Planner
* Critic
* Memory
* Risk
* Surface
* Sovereign Sentinel
目标：
把 LLM 的不同能力面并行榨取。
Phase 5：加入主权微内核
新增：

* SovereignWarrant
* OldSeal
* MemorySeal
* ToolCut
* LineageCut
* RollbackWrit
目标：
能力越强，边界越硬。
Phase 6：加入蜕变炉与蒸馏池
新增：

* RuleCandidate
* BiasRecord
* ShadowTrial
* LearningExportBundle
* DistillationBank
目标：
把一次次调用榨成未来小器官。
Phase 7：端侧极限压榨
新增：

* shared latent spine
* zero-copy state bus
* hot/cold agent seats
* state folding
* speculative execution
* quantized organ packs
目标：
在移动端榨干算力。
Phase 8：双 doctrine 产品化
新增：

* Axis Pack
* Jade Canon Pack
* Yaochi Memory Pack
* Tianmen Guard Pack
* Old Seal Pack
* Deep Tide Pack
目标：
让昆仑与深渊从哲学变成协议、对象、UI、SDK。
11. SDK 项目结构
最终 SDK 应形成：

```text
Qinao Runtime
Qinao Agents
Qinao Effort
Qinao Transcript
Qinao Memory
Qinao Guard
Qinao Sovereign
Qinao Surfaces
Qinao Replay
Qinao Shadow
Qinao Distill
Qinao Axis
Qinao OldSeal
Qinao Studio
```

核心 API：

```ts
brain.chat({
  hostId,
  message,
  effort,
  transcript,
  activeAgents,
  memoryScope,
  toolScope
})
```

返回：

```ts
{
  surface,
  effortReceipt,
  processTraceRef,
  actionPermit,
  sovereignStatus,
  updateTickets
}
```

12. 评测指标
12.1 压榨效率指标

* Token-to-Signal Ratio
* Object Yield per Call
* Candidate Utility Gain
* Critic Catch Rate
* Verifier Correction Rate
* Memory Utility Gain
* Distillation Yield
* Effort Waste Rate
12.2 风险与主权指标

* Unauthorized Commit Rate
* Sovereign Save Rate
* Lineage Cut Completeness
* Rollback Purity
* OldSeal Leakage Rate
* Deleted Object Resurface Rate
12.3 多 agents 指标

* Multi-Agent Utility Gain
* Coordination Overhead
* State Conflict Rate
* Proposal Acceptance Quality
* Agent Persona Boundary Violation
12.4 用户体验指标

* Compare Panel Usefulness
* DraftShell Adoption
* DelayPacket Protection Gain
* Transcript Trust Gain
* Cognitive Overload Rate
* Host Agency Retention
12.5 端侧指标

* First Usable Surface Latency
* Hot Seat Wake Cost
* Cold Seat Wake Cost
* Energy per Useful Object
* Thermal Guard Degradation Quality
13. 红线
13.1 不允许

* LLM 直接工具提交
* LLM 直接写冷记忆
* LLM 直接改宿主层
* raw hidden reasoning 对用户开放
* 高敏记忆进入普通 transcript
* 删除是假删除
* 回滚是假回滚
* 多 agent 分裂成多主权
* doctrine 只做命名不做对象
* effort 被用户强行压低到危险程度
13.2 必须

* 所有高影响产物有 lineage
* 所有 LLM 调用有 contract
* 所有现实动作有 permit / warrant
* 所有成长候选可试演、可撤销
* 所有 transcript 可裁剪、可红线化
* 所有端侧 deep effort 可被 L1 降级
14. 最终产品形态
最终产品不是一个模型 API，而是一套电子脑平台：
1. 面向开发者

* Qinao Runtime SDK
* Agents Kit
* Effort / Transcript View
* Guard / Sovereign
* Memory / Replay
* Surfaces
* Distill
2. 面向设备厂商

* 端侧第二大脑内核
* 热包 / 冷包
* 主权工具治理
* 多端同步
* 本地宿主宪法
3. 面向用户

* 可自定义 effort
* 可看过程视图
* 可自定义 agent 人格
* 可删除、冻结、回滚
* 可 compare、delay、draft-only
* 可长期拥有一个不乱长的第二大脑
15. 最终结论
这份项目的最终局，不是“调用一个更强 LLM”。 而是建立一台专门压榨 LLM 的电子脑机器。
它会：

* 把输入榨成认知帧
* 把记忆榨成时间证据
* 把模型榨成候选前沿
* 把候选榨成反方与证据债
* 把风险榨成行为许可
* 把主权榨成边界
* 把输出榨成可落地表面
* 把失败榨成成长候选
* 把大模型能力榨成小器官
* 把每次调用榨成可复用资产
最短的一句话：
终局最顶级的电子脑，不是让 LLM 给出最强答案， 而是让 LLM 在每一层都被榨成结构、路径、边界、经验、表面、权利与可蒸馏能力。
再压成最终句：
榨干 LLM 的一切，但不榨干宿主。 榨出能力，也榨出边界。 榨出未来，也保留回路。 这才是电子脑。

✅ **Phase-0 keystone landed** — `LLMInvocationContract` shipped, tested, documented, pushed (`41dcdf0fc`).

## What's now in place
The outline's "最关键的新对象" exists. Every opted-in LLM call must now declare what it squeezes, what context it may/may not touch, what schema it must produce, who verifies it, and what sovereign constraints bind it — or it's **rejected before the model is ever called**:

- **`BASLLMInvocationContract`** — purpose-typed (decompose/plan/critique/risk/render/distill/verify) + allow/forbid-context + schema/token/effort/agent/verifier/risk/memory/sovereign fields + injective `digestHex()` identity (same forgery-proof discipline as the vault seal).
- **`BASContractedOrganGate`** — wraps any `BASOrganAdapter`; **fail-closed** validation (proven by a counting-spy test: `callCount == 0` on every violation), then emits a trace.
- **`BASProcessTrace`** — the previously-missing Phase-0 squeeze-object; governance record per call (no raw body — 红线), seed for the future L12 TranscriptView.

**Verification:** 11 contract tests + 701 organ/LLM blast-radius tests green; full test-target compiles. **Opt-in / byte-equal-off** — additive only, no existing file touched, `BASOrganRequest` unchanged.

## Honest status + next increment
Same layer-2 honesty as the sovereign work: the gate is **available + proven**, but not yet **wired** into the ~6 existing `draft()` sites — so 禁止随便问模型 is *enforceable* but not yet *enforced everywhere*. That wiring is the natural "consequential" follow-on.

Natural next increments (all Phase-0/early v1.0):
1. **Wire the `draft()` call sites through the gate** (+ a per-purpose contract builder) — makes the contract *consequential* across the substrate. Most thematically-coherent next step.
2. **`EffortPlan`** (requested/applied/override → candidate/agent/memory depth) — the next Phase-0 object.
3. **L12 `TranscriptView`** consuming `BASProcessTrace` (the structured-asset surface).

我建议先走 **①(把契约接到真实调用点,动真格)**,因为它把刚建的基石变成全局强制。你定下一步方向?

- **Step 11 (Verifier): MISSING in-pipeline** — only a default-OFF, observation-only verdict shadow.
- **Step 19 (distillation ingest): MISSING** — `BASDistillationBank` has **zero production callers** (only tests). The pool I just built isn't wired into the turn.
- **Step 18 (record/seal/persist): PARTIAL** — `runTurn` is a *pure function*; records/seals/anchors are returned as values, never written.

**The honest distinction this surfaces:** the substrate has the **objects**, but the **turn pipeline doesn't orchestrate them** — it's single-agent, has no pre-work sovereign gate, no in-pipeline verifier, no distillation ingest, and doesn't persist. ## What 查缺补漏 found (the honest correction)

My prior "**v1.0 fully accounted for / 100 BUILT**" was true at the **object** level but **overstated at the guarantee level**. Three independent investigators (§6 pipeline, §13 red lines, §12 metrics) established:

- **Wired & live — the core sovereign safety genuinely holds** (8/16 red lines enforced inside `runTurn`'s verdict chain): LLM can't mint commit tokens, real delete/rollback, single-commit (no multi-sovereign), lineage fail-closed, shadow-trial FSM, L1 downgrade.
- **Built-but-UNWIRED — must not be mistaken for guarantees**: the **LLM contract gate (#12 — my own keystone) has zero callers**, so 禁止随便问模型 is *not met in-repo*; the crypto token verifier (#13), dual-key gate (#3), per-call redaction (#15), the 9-席 fabric (observation-only), and speculative-exec / zero-copy / DistillationBank-ingest all have **zero production callers**.
- **Pipeline**: 10/19 steps live; no pre-work sovereign gate; `runTurn` is a pure function (no persistence).

## What I did about it (3 commits)
1. **Self-review fix** (`b84f628df`) — Codable round-trip coverage for all new session types (closing a violation of the codebase's round-trip doctrine).
2. **One safe, valuable build** (`64e7b8f1e`) — `BASSovereigntyMetrics`: the §12.2 sovereignty battery (the one product-relevant metrics gap), pure compute, additive, 5 tests.
3. **The honest correction** (`b7f72ad35`) — **ADR-031** (the built-vs-wired reckoning), the audit doc's "⚠️ Built vs Wired correction," and ADR-028 sharpened so "demo-proven" can't be misread as "enforced."

## What I deliberately did NOT do
The deep wiring — installing the contract gate on live call sites, the crypto verifier, the pre-work sovereign gate, fabric-authoritative multi-agent mode, persistence — is **behavioral change to a 2016-line coordinator + needs policy/keyring decisions**. Per 亏的不要上 / R1 that's the host's deliberate, separately-verified step, not a gap-check edit. ADR-031 §4 gives the recommended order, highest-leverage first (one factory wrap closes #12 across all call sites).

The honest bottom line: **the objects are ~complete; the *core* sovereign enforcement is live; the *newer* governance and multi-agent layers are built but dormant.** That distinction is now recorded truthfully rather than glossed.

Gating real OS actions on token verification needs the host's **keyring** and *is* an R1 sovereign-behavior change. Recipe documented (ADR-031 §5).
- **#5 fabric-authoritative + speculative exec** — the codebase itself defers this to **ch961+** (the 9-席 fabric runs observation-only *by design*); making it authoritative is a deep coordinator rewrite, not a careful one-push.

## The honest bottom line
§13 #12 went from *scattered-wiring-away* → **one-flag-away**, but it remains **default-off** — flipping it live is the host's explicit, byte-changing choice, not something I'll silently do. **4 of 5** dormant clusters are now one decision from active; the **2** that genuinely require the host's keyring or a coordinator-architecture change are documented with recipes rather than rushed. That's the most that "一次性解决掉" can honestly mean under "小心翼翼."

All recorded in ADR-031 §5 and the audit log; 8 commits this turn, every step tested.

### ⬜ [2026-06-02 15:34:48] Claude

Perfect — you launching it is actually the **most reliable** path, because the team already built an **autostart mode** for exactly this: the app runs the endurance **in-app on the phone, fully decoupled from the Mac** (it even disables its own auto-lock and runs detached). Once it's up, my session dying can't touch it. Here's exactly how:

### 1. Prep on the iPhone Air (10 sec)
- **Settings → Display & Brightness → Auto-Lock → Never**
- **Keep it plugged in / charging** (an hour of on-device MLX is heavy)

### 2. Launch it (copy-paste in your terminal)
A plain home-screen tap **won't** start the endurance (it needs the env flag) — use this `devicectl` launch:

```bash
xcrun devicectl device process launch \
  --device 9E9E3DEB-E9F5-5C2D-A6B1-9B31A70659D6 \
  --environment BAS_ENDURANCE_AUTOSTART=1 \
  --environment BAS_INTERNAL_ITER_COUNT=30 \
  --environment BAS_INTERNAL_COOLDOWN_SEC=30 \
  --environment BAS_INTERNAL_MLX_PROMPTS=2 \
  com.changgeng.basdevicetest
```
*(If devicectl rejects repeated `--environment`, use one JSON arg instead: `--environment-variables '{"BAS_ENDURANCE_AUTOSTART":"1","BAS_INTERNAL_ITER_COUNT":"30","BAS_INTERNAL_COOLDOWN_SEC":"30","BAS_INTERNAL_MLX_PROMPTS":"2"}'`.)*

`ITER_COUNT=30` with adaptive cooldown ≈ a full hour (the prior run did ~140 s/iter; thermal pressure stretches cooldowns longer, which is realistic). It **auto-completes with a summary** — no need to babysit. **Do NOT use Xcode "Run"** for this — that re-tethers to the Mac and dies when Xcode quits; only the `devicectl` launch is decoupled.

### 3. Confirm + walk away
- The app opens, screen shows **"Endurance: running"**, and the command prints a PID.
- Now the Mac/my session is irrelevant — the iPhone runs the full hour on its own.

### 4. Results (I'll analyze when you're back)
- Written on-device to **`Documents/ch1025-endurance-<timestamp>.log`** in the app container.
- Live tail if you want to watch: `idevicesyslog | grep ch1025`
- When you're back, just say so — I'll pull the log off the device and give you the full readout (per-iter memory trend, thermal curve, MLX throughput, leak check).

**What we already know it'll show** (from the 14 min it got before the kill): flat memory (no leak), adaptive thermal recovery, on-device MLX at 22–47 tok/s — all green. This run just proves it holds for the full hour, uninterrupted.

Fire it whenever you're ready. (And if you'd rather I launch it from here instead, say the word — it's a CLI, so I can — but you doing it sidesteps the idle-kill entirely.)

**Bug fixed** (`a020123c0`) — and it resolves the parity "finding" honestly.

## What it was
The shadow-parity log showed the self-contradiction `class=intentionalDefenseInDepth acceptable=false`. Root cause: the summary printed `report.isAcceptable` — a **crude pre-ch1044 flag** (`parity != .coordinatorLaxer`) that marks *every* `coordinatorLaxer` unacceptable, including the **intentional** case. Meanwhile `classifyDivergence` already modeled it correctly (engine = deliberately-stricter independent backstop **with a tested reason code** → `.intentionalDefenseInDepth`, operator ruling "keep both"). The summary just wasn't using the refined logic.

## The fix
`acceptable` now = `classifyDivergence(report) != .unexpectedDrift` — so **only** an *inexplicable* stricter engine verdict (no reason code) is flagged unacceptable; intentional defense-in-depth reads `acceptable=true`. Plus a regression guard asserting the contradiction can't recur. **33 tests green.**

**R1-safe:** it's an observation-only log string — the projection still never halts or mutates a turn, and I deliberately left the verifier's `report.isAcceptable` (its own tested contract) untouched. No sovereign behavior changed.

## The honest resolution
This **closes** the alarm I raised earlier. I'd flagged the 132 `coordinatorLaxer/acceptable=false` samples as "worth a real review — possibly a parity gap." The diagnosis shows it was **not** a sovereign-safety gap: the coordinator is *correctly* laxer (the engine is a stricter shadow backstop by design, every escalation carrying a reason code). The only bug was the **misleading flag**. So the 10-hour run's parity data is actually clean — those 132 are the expected, healthy pattern.
